Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on discrete_math structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 425. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 316 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the discrete_math space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 425 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 316. QED.
